| 258 | Applies to air-brake foundation assemblies on tractors and trailers. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1408 |  |  |
| 259 | Free stroke and applied stroke must both be measured with the system at full reservoir pressure. Chock the wheels and re | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1409 |  |  |
| 260 | Before you start | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1410 |  |  |
| 261 | Air pressure at or above 90 psi. Engine off. Wheels chocked. Parking brake released and the transmission in neutral. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1411 |  |  |
| 262 | Measuring free stroke | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1412 |  |  |
| 263 | Mark the push rod where it leaves the chamber with the brakes fully released. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1413 |  |  |
| 264 | Using a lever, move the slack adjuster until the shoes contact the drum. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1414 |  |  |
| 265 | Measure between the mark and the chamber face. Free stroke should read between 3/8 in. and 5/8 in. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1415 |  |  |
| 266 | Measuring applied stroke | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1416 |  |  |
| 267 | Have an assistant make a full brake application and hold it. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1417 |  |  |
| 268 | Measure the total travel of the push rod. Compare against the chamber size table on page 2. | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1418 |  |  |
| 269 | Record both figures on the inspection. Travel beyond the readjustment limit is an out-of-service condition under FMCSA A | ../../dvi-new/Digital Inspections V2 - All Screens.dc.html:1419 |  |  |
